- **diagnose-crash**: Diagnose why a program crashed on this machine, from a systemd-coredump core dump. - **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnose bugs, test or build failures, unexpected behavior, and performance regressions with an evidence-first loop. - **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnosis loop for hard bugs and performance regressions. Build a feedback loop that goes red on the bug before forming any theory, then minimise the repro, test 3–5 falsifiable hypotheses, instrument with tagged logs, fix the root cause behind a regression test, and clean up. Use when the user says "diagnose" or "debug this", or reports something broken, throwing, failing, flaky or slow, including a failing test or a broken build. Not for reviewing a diff before merge; use code-quality-review. ([sq-cm/claude-template](https://github.com/sq-cm/claude-template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★3)

- **diagnosing-bugs**: Runs a diagnosis loop for hard bugs and performance regressions that builds a red-capable feedback loop before hypothesising, then fixes at the root.
